### [1] [VeriGraphi: A Multi-Agent Framework of Hierarchical RTL Generation for Large Hardware Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14550)
*Sazzadul Islam,Tasnim Tabassum,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对LLM在生成大规模层次化Verilog代码时面临的上下文丢失、接口幻觉和连线错误等问题，提出了VeriGraphi框架。该框架利用规范锚定的知识图谱作为结构基础，通过渐进式编码模块实现了可靠的层次化RTL生成，并在RISC-V处理器等基准测试中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在生成大型层次化硬件设计（Verilog）时存在显著局限：难以复制人类专家的结构化推理过程；在跨模块时容易丢失上下文；常产生接口幻觉、虚构模块间连线、无法保持结构一致性。随着设计复杂度增加以及规范包含非形式化的文本、图表，这些失败愈发严重。

Method: 提出VeriGraphi框架，核心是引入规范锚定的知识图谱（KG）作为RTL生成流水线的架构基板。首先通过迭代式多智能体分析规范文档，构建HDA知识图谱，显式编码模块层次、端口级接口、连线语义和模块间依赖为一阶图实体和关系，提供确定性的、可机器检查的结构脚手架。随后在知识图谱引导下，渐进式编码模块逐步生成伪代码和可综合RTL，在每个子模块阶段强制执行接口一致性和依赖正确性。

Result: 在来自NIST的三个代表性规范文档及其对应实现的基准上进行评估，并提供了RV32I处理器的详细案例研究。结果表明VeriGraphi能够以最少的人工干预实现可靠的层次化RTL生成（包括RISC-V），同时保持良好的功能正确性。

Conclusion: VeriGraphi通过知识图谱驱动的结构化方法，成功解决了LLM生成层次化硬件设计的关键挑战，在最小化人工干预的情况下实现了可靠的RISC-V RTL生成，为大语言模型生成的硬件设计树立了重要里程碑。

Abstract: Generating synthesizable Verilog for large, hierarchical hardware designs remains a significant challenge for large language models (LLMs), which struggle to replicate the structured reasoning that human experts employ when translating complex specifications into RTL. When tasked with producing hierarchical Verilog, LLMs frequently lose context across modules, hallucinate interfaces, fabricate inter-module wiring, and fail to maintain structural coherence - failures that intensify as design complexity grows and specifications involve informal prose, figures, and tables that resist direct operationalization. To address these challenges, we present VeriGraphi, a framework that introduces a spec-anchored Knowledge Graph as the architectural substrate driving the RTL generation pipeline. VeriGraphi constructs a HDA, a structured knowledge graph that explicitly encodes module hierarchy, port-level interfaces, wiring semantics, and inter-module dependencies as first-class graph entities and relations. Built through iterative multi-agent analysis of the specification, this Knowledge Graph provides a deterministic, machine-checkable structural scaffold before code generation. Guided by the KG, a progressive coding module incrementally generates pseudo-code and synthesizable RTL while enforcing interface consistency and dependency correctness at each submodule stage. We evaluate VeriGraphi on a benchmark of three representative specification documents from the National Institute of Standards and Technology and their corresponding implementations, and we present a RV32I processor as a detailed case study to illustrate the full pipeline. The results demonstrate that VeriGraphi enables reliable hierarchical RTL generation with minimal human intervention for RISC-V, marking a significant milestone for LLM-generated hardware design while maintaining strong functional correctness.

</details>


### [2] [Accelerating CRONet on AMD Versal AIE-ML Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14700)
*Kaustubh Mhatre,Vedant Tewari,Aditya Ray,Farhan Khan,Ridwan Olabiyi,Ashif Iquebal,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 首次在AMD Versal AIE-ML架构上实现了拓扑优化神经网络（CRONNet）的端到端硬件加速，通过全片上数据驻留策略，相比同功耗GPU实现了最高2.49倍延迟降低和4.18倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化对于关键基础设施（如桥梁、建筑）的结构健康监测及数字孪生应用至关重要，传统基于有限元分析（FEA）的方法计算量大，而现有的深度神经网络（DNN）替代方案虽减少了计算时间，但在推理级GPU上实现时仍面临高延迟和能效低下的问题。

Method: 提出了一种在AMD Versal AI Engine-ML（AIE-ML）架构上的硬件加速实现方案。该方法高效利用AIE-ML引擎的并行性和存储层次结构优化神经网络算子执行。关键创新点是实现了首个完全在AIE-ML阵列上实现的端到端神经网络，推理过程中所有中间激活值和网络权重均驻留在片上，消除了对DRAM的依赖。

Result: 实验结果表明，在同等功耗预算下（对比Nvidia T4 GPU），经过技术节点缩放后，该实现实现了高达2.49倍的延迟改进和高达4.18倍的能效改进。

Conclusion: 基于Versal AIE-ML的加速方案具有实现低延迟、高能效拓扑优化的潜力，为关键基础设施应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Topology optimization is a computational method used to determine the optimal material distribution within a prescribed design domain, aiming to minimize structural weight while satisfying load and boundary conditions. For critical infrastructure applications, such as structural health monitoring of bridges and buildings, particularly in digital twin contexts, low-latency energy-efficient topology optimization is essential. Traditionally, topology optimization relies on finite element analysis (FEA), a computationally intensive process. Recent advances in deep neural networks (DNNs) have introduced data driven alternatives to FEA, substantially reducing computation time while maintaining solution quality. These DNNs have complex architectures and implementing them on inference-class GPUs results in high latency and poor energy efficiency. To address this challenge, we present a hardware accelerated implementation of a topology optimization neural network (CRONet) on the AMD Versal AI Engine-ML (AIE-ML) architecture. Our approach efficiently exploits the parallelism and memory hierarchy of AIE-ML engines to optimize the execution of various neural network operators. We are the first to implement an end-to-end neural network fully realized on the AIE-ML array, where all intermediate activations and network weights reside on-chip throughout inference, eliminating any reliance on DRAM for intermediate data movement. Experimental results demonstrate that our implementation achieves up to 2.49x improvement in latency and up to 4.18x improvement in energy efficiency compared to an inference-class ML-optimized GPU in the same power budget (Nvidia T4) after scaling for technology node. These results highlight the potential of Versal AIE-ML based acceleration for enabling low-latency energy-efficient topology optimization.

</details>


### [3] [Autonomous Evolution of EDA Tools: Multi-Agent Self-Evolved ABC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15082)
*Cunxi Yu,Haoxing Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于LLM代理的自进化逻辑综合框架，通过自主改进ABC系统的源代码，实现了超越人工启发式的综合策略发现和QoR提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逻辑综合系统依赖人工设计的启发式算法，其优化空间受限于人类经验。为了突破这一限制，研究旨在构建一个能够自主学习和进化的系统，使其能够发现超越人工设计的优化策略，从而持续提升EDA工具的质量。

Method: 提出了一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）代理的自进化逻辑综合框架，作用于完整的ABC代码库。系统首先利用现有的开源综合组件（覆盖流程调优、逻辑最小化和工艺映射）进行引导，随后由LLM代理团队根据“编程指导”提示，在统一的正确性和QoR驱动评估循环下，迭代重写和进化ABC的特定子组件。每个进化周期包括代码修改、编译集成二进制文件、正确性验证以及在多套基准测试（ISCAS 85/89/99、VTR、EPFL、IWLS 2005）上评估QoR。

Result: 通过持续反馈，系统发现了超越人工启发式的优化方案，有效学习了新的综合策略并提升了QoR。实验结果表明，该框架能够在百万行代码规模上自主且渐进式地改进EDA工具。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了基于LLM的自进化框架在大规模EDA工具自主优化方面的可行性与有效性，为逻辑综合系统的自动化演进提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces the first \emph{self-evolving} logic synthesis framework, which leverages Large Language Model (LLM) agents to autonomously improve the source code of \textsc{ABC}, the widely adopted logic synthesis system. Our framework operates on the \emph{entire integrated ABC codebase}, and the output repository preserves its single-binary execution model and command interface. In the initial evolution cycle, we bootstrap the system using existing prior open-source synthesis components, covering flow tuning, logic minimization, and technology mapping, but without manually injecting new heuristics. On top of this foundation, a team of LLM-based agents iteratively rewrites and evolves specific sub-components of ABC following our ``programming guidance`` prompts under a unified correctness and QoR-driven evaluation loop. Each evolution cycle proposes code modifications, compiles the integrated binary, validates correctness, and evaluates quality-of-results (QoR) on \emph{multi-suite benchmarks including ISCAS~85/89/99, VTR, EPFL, and IWLS~2005}. Through continuous feedback, the system discovers optimizations beyond human-designed heuristics, effectively \emph{learning new synthesis strategies} that enhance QoR. We detail the architecture of this self-improving system, its integration with \textsc{ABC}, and results demonstrating that the framework can autonomously and progressively improve EDA tool at full million-line scale.

</details>


### [4] [SCENIC: Stream Computation-Enhanced SmartNIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15128)
*Benjamin Ramhorst,Maximilian Jakob Heer,Luhao Liu,Heejae Kim,Jonas Dann,Jin-Soo Kim,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SCENIC，一种开源数据中心SmartNIC，解决了现有商业网卡不可编程和研究用网卡性能弱的矛盾，在提供200G高性能网络的同时支持灵活的数据面编程和透明应用集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心SmartNIC存在严峻的权衡困境：商业产品虽具备高带宽和良好的软件兼容性，但缺乏定制化和数据处理卸载能力；而研究型设备虽然可编程，但往往带宽低、功能有限且软件兼容性差，难以满足现代AI数据中心的实际需求。

Method: 将网卡数据路径视为一流流计算 substrate，采用软硬件协同设计。SCENIC实现了200G网络数据路径，支持卸载的TCP/IP和RDMA协议栈及任意流量的回退路径。在传输层之上集成了数据路径流计算单元（SCUs）进行数据处理，配备ARM核心进行控制面操作，并可直接访问GPU和SSD。系统完全集成OS，暴露原生Linux网络和RDMA verb接口。

Result: SCENIC的性能可与商业平台相媲美。通过多种用例验证了其通用性，包括卸载的集合通信以及基于哈希的网络到GPU数据分区等场景，证明了其在保持高性能的同时实现了灵活的可编程性。

Conclusion: 通过共享的软硬件抽象实现紧密的协同设计，可以消除SmartNIC在性能与可编程性之间的硬性权衡。SCENIC成功证明了构建一个既具备商业级性能又拥有研究级灵活性的开源数据中心SmartNIC是可行的。

Abstract: Although modern, AI-centric datacenters heavily rely on SmartNICs, existing devices impose a hard trade-off. Commercial SmartNICs provide high bandwidth and easy software integration, but offer limited support for customization and data processing offload. In contrast, research SmartNICs often suffer from low bandwidth, limited functionality, and poor software compatibility -- to the point that many are not actual NICs in a technical sense. This gap can be closed by treating the NIC datapath as a first-class stream computation substrate with shared hardware/software abstractions for a tight co-design of infrastructure and applications. To demonstrate this, we introduce SCENIC, an open-source datacenter SmartNIC. SCENIC implements a 200G network datapath over offloaded TCP/IP and RDMA stacks, as well as a fallback path for processing arbitrary network traffic. On top of the network logic, SCENIC combines on-datapath Stream Compute Units (SCUs) for data processing and embedded ARM cores for flexible control path manipulation with direct access to GPUs and SSDs. SCENIC is fully integrated with the OS, exposing native Linux network and RDMA verb interfaces, making the programmable datapath transparent to existing applications while enabling control of, e.g., user-defined offloads and programmable congestion control. SCENIC's performance matches commercial platforms, and we show its versatility through several use cases such as offloaded collective communication and network-to-GPU hash-based data partitioning.

</details>


### [5] [Democratization of Real-time Multi-Spectral Photoacoustic Imaging: Open-Sourced System Architecture for OPOTEK Phocus & Verasonics Vantage Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15255)
*Ryo Murakami,Yichuan Tang,Haichong K. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对实时多光谱光声成像中的同步不稳定问题，提出了一种基于独立微控制器和解耦流式框架的开源软硬件架构，实现了确定性激光触发计数并消除了操作系统时序偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时多光谱光声成像（RT-mPAI）在将快速调谐激光器与运行于非实时操作系统的数据采集平台对接时，常面临同步不稳定的技术难题。

Method: 针对广泛采用的OPOTEK Phocus激光器与Verasonics Vantage系统组合，建立了一套开源软硬件架构。该方法采用独立微控制器进行确定性激光触发计数，并结合解耦的客户端-服务器数据流框架，从而绕过操作系统引起的时序偏差和本地存储瓶颈。

Result: 该系统成功规避了操作系统导致的时序偏差与本地存储瓶颈，建立了稳定的RT-mPAI工作流程，并通过开源代码降低了技术门槛与成本。

Conclusion: 通过开源该技术流程并培育协作环境，旨在降低RT-mPAI的技术与成本门槛，推动稳定RT-mPAI研究的普及化，并期望以此促进活跃的开源社区形成。

Abstract: Real-time multi-spectral photoacoustic imaging (RT-mPAI) often suffers from synchronization instabilities when interfacing fast-tuning lasers with data acquisition platforms executing on non-real-time operating systems. To overcome this, we establish an open-source hardware-software architecture tailored for the widely adopted combination of the OPOTEK Phocus lasers and Verasonics Vantage systems. By employing an independent micro-controller for deterministic laser trigger counting alongside a decoupled client-server data streaming framework, the proposed system circumvents OS-induced timing deviations and local storage bottlenecks. By open-sourcing this pipeline and cultivating a collaborative environment to share both code and ideas, we aim to lower the technical and cost barriers for RT-mPAI, thereby democratizing access to stable RT-mPAI research and, more ambitiously, fostering a vibrant open-source community.

</details>

### [6] [The Devil Is in Gradient Entanglement: Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator for Robust Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14176)
*Haiyang Zheng,Nan Pu,Yaqi Cai,Teng Long,Wenjing Li,Nicu Sebe,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对广义类别发现（GCD）中联合优化导致的梯度纠缠问题，提出了即插即用的EAGC模块，通过锚定梯度方向和能量感知投影来调节优化过程，在多个基准上实现了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GCD方法通过联合优化监督和无监督目标取得了一定成果，但存在内在的优化干扰问题。作者通过定量分析发现了关键的梯度纠缠现象：它扭曲了监督梯度并削弱已知类别间的判别性，同时导致已知类别与新类别之间的表示子空间重叠，降低了新类别的可分性。

Method: 提出了能量感知梯度协调器（EAGC），包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于锚定的梯度对齐（AGA）：引入参考模型来锚定有标注样本的梯度方向，保护已知类别的判别结构免受无标注梯度干扰；(2) 能量感知弹性投影（EEP）：将无标注梯度软投影到已知类别子空间的补空间上，并根据与已知子空间的对齐程度自适应缩放投影，减少子空间重叠的同时保留可能属于已知类别的无标注样本。

Result: 实验表明，EAGC能够持续提升现有方法的性能，并在多个基准数据集上建立了新的最先进结果，证明了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 本文首次从梯度层面识别并解决了GCD任务中的优化干扰问题。EAGC作为一个即插即用的梯度级模块，能够有效缓解梯度纠缠现象，在保持已知类别判别性的同时提升新类别的可分性，为广义类别发现任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) leverages labeled data to categorize unlabeled samples from known or unknown classes. Most previous methods jointly optimize supervised and unsupervised objectives and achieve promising results. However, inherent optimization interference still limits their ability to improve further. Through quantitative analysis, we identify a key issue, i.e., gradient entanglement, which 1) distorts supervised gradients and weakens discrimination among known classes, and 2) induces representation-subspace overlap between known and novel classes, reducing the separability of novel categories. To address this issue, we propose the Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator (EAGC), a plug-and-play gradient-level module that explicitly regulates the optimization process. EAGC comprises two components: Anchor-based Gradient Alignment (AGA) and Energy-aware Elastic Projection (EEP). AGA introduces a reference model to anchor the gradient directions of labeled samples, preserving the discriminative structure of known classes against the interference of unlabeled gradients. EEP softly projects unlabeled gradients onto the complement of the known-class subspace and derives an energy-based coefficient to adaptively scale the projection for each unlabeled sample according to its degree of alignment with the known subspace, thereby reducing subspace overlap without suppressing unlabeled samples that likely belong to known classes. Experiments show that EAGC consistently boosts existing methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://haiyangzheng.github.io/EAGC.

</details>


### [7] [MixAtlas: Uncertainty-aware Data Mixture Optimization for Multimodal LLM Midtraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14198)
*Bingbing Wen,Sirajul Salekin,Feiyang Kang,Bill Howe,Lucy Lu Wang,Javier Movellan,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MixAtlas方法，通过双轴分解（图像概念和任务监督）优化多模态数据混合，使用小代理模型和高斯过程搜索策略，在多个基准测试上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态训练方法仅在单一维度（如数据格式或任务类型）上调整数据混合，而域重加权虽能提升样本效率和泛化能力，但在多模态预训练中的数据混合优化研究尚属空白。

Method: MixAtlas沿两个轴分解训练语料库：图像概念（通过CLIP嵌入发现的10个视觉域聚类）和任务监督（包括字幕生成、OCR、定位、检测和VQA等5种目标类型）。使用小型代理模型（Qwen2-0.5B）配合高斯过程代理和GP-UCB采集策略，在相同代理预算下搜索混合空间。

Result: 在Qwen2-7B上，优化的混合配方相比最强基线平均性能提升8.5%-17.6%；在Qwen2.5-7B上提升1.0%-3.3%。两种设置下均以最多2倍更少的训练步数达到基线等效的训练损失。在0.5B代理上发现的配方可迁移至7B规模训练。

Conclusion: MixAtlas成功实现了可检查、可适应且可迁移到新语料库的基准导向数据配方生成，证明了双轴分解和高效搜索策略在多模态数据混合优化中的有效性，为多模态预训练提供了新的数据优化范式。

Abstract: Domain reweighting can improve sample efficiency and downstream generalization, but data-mixture optimization for multimodal midtraining remains largely unexplored. Current multimodal training recipes tune mixtures along a single dimension, typically data format or task type. We introduce MixAtlas, a method that produces benchmark-targeted data recipes that can be inspected, adapted, and transferred to new corpora. MixAtlas decomposes the training corpus along two axes: image concepts (10 visual-domain clusters discovered via CLIP embeddings) and task supervision (5 objective types including captioning, OCR, grounding, detection, and VQA). Using small proxy models (Qwen2-0.5B) paired with a Gaussian-process surrogate and GP-UCB acquisition, MixAtlas searches the resulting mixture space with the same proxy budget as regression-based baselines but finds better-performing mixtures. We evaluate on 10 benchmarks spanning visual understanding, document reasoning, and multimodal reasoning. On Qwen2-7B, optimized mixtures improve average performance by 8.5%-17.6% over the strongest baseline; on Qwen2.5-7B, gains are 1.0%-3.3%. Both settings reach baseline-equivalent training loss in up to 2 times fewer steps. Recipes discovered on 0.5B proxies transfer to 7B-scale training across Qwen model families.

</details>


### [8] [Learning Ad Hoc Network Dynamics via Graph-Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14811)
*Can Karacelebi,Yusuf Talha Sahin,Elif Surer,Ertan Onur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了G-RSSM，一种图结构循环状态空间模型，用于自组网网络动态建模。通过离线轨迹学习节点动态，并在分簇任务中通过想象 rollout 训练策略，在N=30到1000节点的27个场景中仅用N=50训练就实现了高连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组网无线网络表现出复杂的内在耦合动态（节点移动、能量耗尽和拓扑变化），难以进行解析建模。无模型的深度强化学习需要持续的在线交互，而现有的基于模型的方法使用扁平状态表示，丢失了每节点的结构信息。

Method: 提出G-RSSM（图结构循环状态空间模型），通过跨节点多头注意力机制维护每节点的潜在状态，从离线轨迹中联合学习动态。应用于分簇任务，通过在学习的世界模型中进行想象 rollout 来完全训练簇头选择策略。

Result: 在涵盖MANET、VANET、FANET、WSN和战术网络等27个评估场景中（N=30到1000节点），仅用N=50训练的学习策略就能保持高连通性，展现出良好的规模泛化能力。

Conclusion: 这是首个应用于规模不可知无线自组网中组合式每节点决策的多物理图结构世界模型，为处理复杂网络动态提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Ad hoc wireless networks exhibit complex, innate and coupled dynamics: node mobility, energy depletion and topology change that are difficult to model analytically. Model-free deep reinforcement learning requires sustained online interaction whereas existing model based approaches use flat state representations that lose per node structure. Therefore we propose G-RSSM, a graph structured recurrent state space model that maintains per node latent states with cross node multi head attention to learn the dynamics jointly from offline trajectories. We apply the proposed method to the downstream task clustering where a cluster head selection policy trains entirely through imagined rollouts in the learned world model. Across 27 evaluation scenarios spanning MANET, VANET, FANET, WSN and tactical networks with N=30 to 1000 nodes, the learned policy maintains high connectivity with only trained for N=50. Herein, we propose the first multi physics graph structured world model applied to combinatorial per node decision making in size agnostic wireless ad hoc networks.

</details>


### [9] [Portfolio Optimization Proxies under Label Scarcity and Regime Shifts via Bayesian and Deterministic Students under Semi-Supervised Sandwich Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14206)
*Adhiraj Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器学习辅助的投资组合优化框架，通过师生学习管道，在低数据环境下利用合成数据增强训练，使学生模型在性能和鲁棒性上匹配或超越CVaR教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对投资组合优化在低数据环境和市场机制不确定性下面临的挑战，如标注数据稀缺（仅104个观测值）和模型泛化能力不足，本文旨在设计一种能够克服数据限制并提升模型稳健性的方法。

Method: 构建师生学习管道：使用条件风险价值（CVaR）优化器生成监督标签；采用基于因子模型和t-连接树残差生成合成数据以扩充样本；训练贝叶斯和确定性神经网络作为学生模型。实验框架包括控制合成实验、分布内真实市场评估（C2A）和跨宇宙泛化测试（D2A），并采用滚动评估协议，定期微调冻结的预训练模型。

Result: 在真实市场环境中，学生模型在多个设置下匹配或超越CVaR教师模型的性能；在机制转换下表现出更好的鲁棒性；同时降低了投资组合换手率。

Conclusion: 混合优化学习方法能够有效提升低数据受限环境下的投资组合构建效果，证明了合成数据增强与师生学习框架在金融领域的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a machine learning assisted portfolio optimization framework designed for low data environments and regime uncertainty. We construct a teacher student learning pipeline in which a Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) optimizer generates supervisory labels, and neural models (Bayesian and deterministic) are trained using both real and synthetically augmented data. The synthetic data is generated using a factor based model with t copula residuals, enabling training beyond the limited real sample of 104 labeled observations. We evaluate four student models under a structured experimental framework comprising (i) controlled synthetic experiments (3 x 5 seed grid), (ii) in-distribution real market evaluation (C2A) and (iii) cross-universe generalization (D2A). In real-market settings, models are deployed using a rolling evaluation protocol where a frozen pretrained model is periodically fine tuned on recent observations and reset to its base state, ensuring stability while allowing limited adaptation. Results show that student models can match or outperform the CVaR teacher in several settings, while achieving improved robustness under regime shifts and reduced turnover. These findings suggest that hybrid optimization learning approaches can enhance portfolio construction in data constrained environments

</details>


### [10] [ELMoE-3D: Leveraging Intrinsic Elasticity of MoE for Hybrid-Bonding-Enabled Self-Speculative Decoding in On-Premises Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14626)
*Yuseon Choi,Jingu Lee,Jungjun Oh,Sunjoo Whang,Byeongcheol Kim,Minsung Kim,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ELMoE-3D框架，通过硬件-软件协同设计，统一缓存加速与推测解码，在混合键合3D堆叠硬件上实现了跨批处理尺寸的平均6.6倍加速和4.4倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型（MoE）的本地服务受限于内存带宽瓶颈，现有的内存中心架构在低算术强度下计算利用率不足，而推测解码在MoE场景下因验证阶段需加载被拒绝token的专家而收益受限。

Method: 提出ELMoE-3D框架，识别MoE的两个内在弹性轴（专家和比特），构建弹性自推测解码（Elastic-SD）作为专家缓存和自草稿模型；采用LSB增强的比特切片架构，利用比特切片表示的内在冗余支持比特嵌套执行，基于混合键合（HB）3D堆叠硬件实现。

Result: 在批处理尺寸1-16范围内，相比xPU上的朴素MoE服务实现平均6.6倍加速和4.4倍能效提升；相比最优先加速器基准实现2.2倍加速和1.4倍能效提升。

Conclusion: ELMoE-3D通过统一缓存加速与推测解码，并利用3D堆叠硬件的高带宽优势，成功解决了MoE服务在不同批处理尺寸下的性能瓶颈，实现了显著的性能与能效提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become the dominant architecture for large-scale language models, yet on-premises serving remains fundamentally memory-bound as batching turns sparse per-token compute into dense memory activation. Memory-centric architectures (PIM, NMP) improve bandwidth but leave compute underutilized under MoE's low arithmetic intensity at high batch sizes. Speculative decoding (SD) trades idle compute for fewer target invocations, yet verification must load experts even for rejected tokens, severely limiting its benefit in MoE especially at low batch sizes. We propose ELMoE-3D, a hybrid-bonding (HB)-based HW-SW co-designed framework that unifies cache-based acceleration and speculative decoding to offer overall speedup across batch sizes. We identify two intrinsic elasticity axes of MoE-expert and bit-and jointly scale them to construct Elastic Self-Speculative Decoding (Elastic-SD), which serves as both an expert cache and a strongly aligned self-draft model accelerated by high HB bandwidth. Our LSB-augmented bit-sliced architecture exploits inherent redundancy in bit-slice representations to natively support bit-nested execution. On our 3D-stacked hardware, ELMoE-3D achieves an average $6.6\times$ speedup and $4.4\times$ energy efficiency gain over naive MoE serving on xPU across batch sizes 1-16, and delivers $2.2\times$ speedup and $1.4\times$ energy efficiency gain over the best-performing prior accelerator baseline.

</details>


### [11] [Towards Verified and Targeted Explanations through Formal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14209)
*Hanchen David Wang,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Preston K. Robinette,Ipek Oguz,Taylor T. Johnson,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ViTaX框架，首个能提供形式化保证的可解释AI方法，针对用户指定的关键目标类别生成带有数学保证的半事实解释，在保证解释质量的同时显著提升了保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法存在局限性：启发式归因技术（如LIME、Integrated Gradients）缺乏数学保证；形式化验证方法虽能验证鲁棒性但无针对性，分析最近的决策边界而不管其是否代表关键风险。在安全关键系统中，并非所有误分类后果相同，例如将“停止”标志误认为“60公里/小时”比误认为“禁止超车”危险得多。

Method: 提出ViTaX（Verified and Targeted Explanations）框架，针对给定输入（类别y）和用户指定的关键替代类别（类别t）：(1)识别对y→t转换最敏感的最小特征子集；(2)应用形式化可达性分析，保证对这些特征进行ε扰动不会将分类翻转为t。形式化定义为“Targeted epsilon-Robustness”，用于认证特征子集在面向特定目标类别的扰动下是否保持鲁棒。

Result: 在MNIST、GTSRB、EMNIST和TaxiNet数据集上的评估表明，ViTaX在最小化解释基数的同时，保真度提升超过30%。这是首个能为模型针对用户指定替代类别的抗干扰能力提供形式化保证的解释方法。

Conclusion: ViTaX填补了可解释性与形式化保证之间的空白，为安全关键领域提供了可信赖且可解释的方法，使利益相关者能够获得既有数学保证又针对特定风险目标的模型解释。

Abstract: As deep neural networks are deployed in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and medical diagnosis, stakeholders need explanations that are interpretable but also trustworthy with formal guarantees. Existing XAI methods fall short: heuristic attribution techniques (e.g., LIME, Integrated Gradients) highlight influential features but offer no mathematical guarantees about decision boundaries, while formal methods verify robustness yet remain untargeted, analyzing the nearest boundary regardless of whether it represents a critical risk. In safety-critical systems, not all misclassifications carry equal consequences; confusing a "Stop" sign for a "60 kph" sign is far more dangerous than confusing it with a "No Passing" sign. We introduce ViTaX (Verified and Targeted Explanations), a formal XAI framework that generates targeted semifactual explanations with mathematical guarantees. For a given input (class y) and a user-specified critical alternative (class t), ViTaX: (1) identifies the minimal feature subset most sensitive to the y->t transition, and (2) applies formal reachability analysis to guarantee that perturbing these features by epsilon cannot flip the classification to t. We formalize this through Targeted epsilon-Robustness, certifying whether a feature subset remains robust under perturbation toward a specific target class. ViTaX is the first method to provide formally guaranteed explanations of a model's resilience against user-identified alternatives. Evaluations on MNIST, GTSRB, EMNIST, and TaxiNet demonstrate over 30% fidelity improvement with minimal explanation cardinality.

</details>


### [12] [Shapley Value-Guided Adaptive Ensemble Learning for Explainable Financial Fraud Detection with U.S. Regulatory Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14231)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Md Munna Aziz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对金融犯罪检测中AI模型的“黑箱”问题，评估了解释性质量，提出了SHAP引导的自适应集成模型（SGAE），并对比了LSTM、Transformer和GNN-GraphSAGE三种架构，发现XGBoost解释稳定性最佳，SGAE预测性能最优，GNN-GraphSAGE综合表现良好，且所有结果均符合监管合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪每年给美国机构造成超过320亿美元损失。尽管AI欺诈检测工具日益先进，但其在实际系统中仍面临“黑箱”问题，无法提供监管法规（如OCC Bulletin 2011-12和Federal Reserve SR 11-7）所要求的透明、可审计的解释。这阻碍了AI模型的实际部署与应用。

Method: 1. 系统评估解释质量：采用忠实度（k=5、10、15时的充分性和全面性）和稳定性（30次Bootstrap样本的Kendall's W）指标，对比XGBoost+TreeExplainer与LSTM+DeepExplainer等方法。\n2. 提出SHAP引导的自适应集成方法（SGAE）：基于SHAP归因一致性动态调整每个交易的集成权重。\n3. 完整评估三种深度学习架构：LSTM、Transformer和GNN-GraphSAGE，在完整的590,540条IEEE-CIS交易数据集上进行实验。\n4. 将所有实验结果映射到OCC、SR 11-7和BSA-AML监管合规要求。

Result: 1. 解释性评估：XGBoost+TreeExplainer实现近乎完美的稳定性（W=0.9912），而LSTM+DeepExplainer表现较弱（W=0.4962）。\n2. SGAE模型：在所有测试模型中取得最高AUC-ROC（保留集0.8837；交叉验证0.9245）。\n3. 架构对比：GNN-GraphSAGE表现最佳，AUC-ROC达0.9248，F1分数为0.6013。\n4. 合规映射：所有结果均直接对应OCC、SR 11-7和BSA-AML监管合规要求。

Conclusion: 本研究通过全面评估解释性质量、提出高性能SGAE集成方法以及对比多种深度学习架构，有效解决了金融欺诈检测AI模型的透明性与合规性挑战。结果表明XGBoost具有最佳解释稳定性，SGAE和GNN-GraphSAGE在预测性能上领先，且研究框架可直接支持监管合规审计，为金融机构实际部署可解释AI系统提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Financial crime costs U.S. institutions over $32 billion each year. Although AI tools for fraud detection have become more advanced, their use in real-world systems still faces a major obstacle: many of these models operate as black boxes that cannot provide the transparent, auditable explanations required by regulations such as OCC Bulletin 2011-12 and Federal Reserve SR 11-7. This study makes three main contributions. First, it offers a thorough evaluation of explanation quality across faithfulness (sufficiency and comprehensiveness at k=5, 10, and 15) and stability (Kendall's W across 30 bootstrap samples). XGBoost paired with TreeExplainer achieves near-perfect stability (W=0.9912), while LSTM with DeepExplainer shows weak results (W=0.4962). Second, the paper introduces the SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE), which dynamically adjusts per-transaction ensemble weights based on SHAP attribution agreement, achieving the highest AUC-ROC among all tested models (0.8837 held-out; 0.9245 cross-validation). Third, a complete three-architecture evaluation of LSTM, Transformer, and GNN-GraphSAGE on the full 590,540-transaction IEEE-CIS dataset is provided, with GNN-GraphSAGE achieving AUC-ROC 0.9248 and F1=0.6013. All results are mapped directly to OCC, SR 11-7, and BSA-AML regulatory compliance requirements.

</details>


### [13] [Explainable Graph Neural Networks for Interbank Contagion Surveillance: A Regulatory-Aligned Framework for the U.S. Banking Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14232)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ST-GAT框架，用于美国银行系统困境早期预警和宏观审慎监管，在8,103家FDIC参保机构的数据集上达到0.939的AUPRC，仅次于XGBoost，并识别出ROA和NPL Ratio为关键预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对银行系统性风险早期预警和宏观审慎监管需求，开发可解释的图神经网络方法来监测银行间网络的时空动态特征，实现困境银行早期预警。

Method: 提出时空图注意力网络(ST-GAT)框架，基于FDIC Call Reports数据，使用最大熵估计重建双边敞口构建动态有向加权图，结合BiLSTM时序组件和注意力机制建模8,103家机构在58个季度(2010Q1-2024Q2)的时空演化特征。

Result: 模型达到0.939±0.010的AUPRC，在所有GNN架构中表现最优，仅略低于XGBoost(0.944)。消融分析显示BiLSTM时序组件贡献+0.020 AUPRC，时序注意力权重呈现单调递减模式。置换重要性分析识别ROA(0.309)和NPL比率(0.252)为主要预测因子，与2023年区域性银行危机事后分析一致。

Conclusion: ST-GAT框架成功实现了可解释的银行困境早期预警，模型性能优异且具有明确的经济学解释性，为银行系统性风险监测提供了有效的GNN解决方案，所有数据和代码已公开发布。

Abstract: The Spatial-Temporal Graph Attention Network (ST-GAT) framework was created to serve as an explainable GNN-based solution for detecting bank distress early warning signs and for conducting macro-prudential surveillance of the interbank system in the United States. The ST-GAT framework models 8,103 FDIC insured institutions across 58 quarterly snapshots (2010Q1-2024Q2). Bilateral exposures were reconstructed from publicly available FDIC Call Reports using maximum entropy estimation to produce a dynamic directed weighted graph. The framework achieves the highest AUPRC among all GNN architectures (0.939 +/- 0.010), trailing only XGBoost (0.944). Ablation analysis confirms the BiLSTM temporal component contributes +0.020 AUPRC; temporal attention weights exhibit a monotonically decreasing pattern consistent with long-run structural vulnerability weighting. Permutation importance identifies ROA (0.309) and NPL Ratio (0.252) as dominant predictors, consistent with post-mortem analyses of the 2023 regional banking crisis. All data are publicly available FDIC Call Reports and FRED series; all code and results are released.

</details>


### [14] [Graph-Based Fraud Detection with Dual-Path Graph Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14235)
*Wei He,Wensheng Gan,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPF-GFD模型，通过双路径图滤波解决欺诈图中关系伪装、高异质性和类别不平衡问题，在四个真实金融欺诈检测数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈图固有的关系伪装、高异质性和类别不平衡特性导致大多数图神经网络（GNN）在欺诈检测任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出双路径图滤波欺诈检测模型（DPF-GFD）。首先对原始图应用Beta小波算子捕获结构模式，然后构建基于距离的相似性图并应用改进的低通滤波器，最后通过监督表示学习融合两条路径的嵌入，并使用集成树模型评估欺诈风险。

Result: 在四个真实世界金融欺诈检测数据集上的全面实验证明了该方法的有效性，相比现有方法能获得更具区分性和稳定性的节点表示。

Conclusion: DPF-GFD通过频率互补的双路径滤波范式，显式解耦结构异常建模和特征相似性建模，有效解决了高异质性和类别不平衡欺诈图中的挑战。

Abstract: Fraud detection on graph data can be viewed as a demanding task that requires distinguishing between different types of nodes. Because graph neural networks (GNNs) are naturally suited for processing information encoded in graph form through their message-passing operations, methods based on GNN models have increasingly attracted attention in the fraud detection domain. However, fraud graphs inherently exhibit relation camouflage, high heterophily, and class imbalance, causing most GNNs to underperform in fraud detection tasks. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a Graph-Based Fraud Detection Model with Dual-Path Graph Filtering (DPF-GFD). DPF-GFD first applies a beta wavelet-based operator to the original graph to capture key structural patterns. It then constructs a similarity graph from distance-based node representations and applies an improved low-pass filter. The embeddings from the original and similarity graphs are fused through supervised representation learning to obtain node features, which are finally used by an ensemble tree model to assess the fraud risk of unlabeled nodes. Unlike existing single-graph smoothing approaches, DPF-GFD introduces a frequency-complementary dual-path filtering paradigm tailored for fraud detection, explicitly decoupling structural anomaly modeling and feature similarity modeling. This design enables more discriminative and stable node representations in highly heterophilous and imbalanced fraud graphs. Comprehensive experiments on four real-world financial fraud detection datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>


### [15] [TOPCELL: Topology Optimization of Standard Cell via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14237)
*Zhan Song,Yu-Tung Liu,Chen Chen,Guoheng Sun,Jiaqi Yin,Chia-tung Ho,Ang Li,Haoxing Ren,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TOPCELL框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）将晶体管拓扑优化建模为生成任务，在2nm工艺节点实验中表现优异，并在7nm库生成任务中以85.91倍加速达到穷举求解器的布局质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体管拓扑优化是标准单元设计的关键步骤，决定了扩散共享效率和后续布线能力。然而，随着先进工艺节点电路复杂度的增加，传统穷举搜索方法计算成本过高，寻找最优拓扑成为持续的性能瓶颈。

Method: 引入TOPCELL框架，将高维拓扑探索重构为基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成任务。采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）对模型进行微调，使其拓扑优化策略与逻辑（电路）约束和空间（布局）约束相对齐。

Result: 在面向2nm工艺节点的工业流程实验中，TOPCELL在发现可布线且具有物理感知的拓扑方面显著优于基础模型；在集成至最先进的7nm库生成自动化流程时，展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，在匹配穷举求解器布局质量的同时实现了85.91倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了利用大语言模型进行晶体管拓扑优化的可行性与高效性，能够以数量级的速度提升解决复杂的拓扑搜索问题，为先进工艺节点的标准单元自动化设计提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Transistor topology optimization is a critical step in standard cell design, directly dictating diffusion sharing efficiency and downstream routability. However, identifying optimal topologies remains a persistent bottleneck, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity in advanced nodes. This paper introduces TOPCELL, a novel and scalable framework that reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using Large Language Models (LLMs). We employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune the model, aligning its topology optimization strategy with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints. Experimental results within an industrial flow targeting an advanced 2nm technology node demonstrate that TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. When integrated into a state-of-the-art (SOTA) automation flow for a 7nm library generation task, TOPCELL exhibits robust zero-shot generalization and matches the layout quality of exhaustive solvers while achieving an 85.91x speedup.

</details>


### [16] [Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14243)
*Sourav Ganguly,Kartik Pandit,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习，提出算法RHC-UCRL，在对抗者影响状态转移的环境中实现了次线性的遗憾与约束违反保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策系统中的状态转移常受外部不可控因素（如竞争者、环境扰动或策略性对手）影响。忽略这些因素会导致看似最优的策略在部署时发生灾难性失败，尤其当系统需要满足安全约束时。现有的约束MDP和鲁棒RL方法未能显式建模智能体与外部因素之间的策略交互。

Method: 将外部因素建模为共同决定状态转移的对抗策略π̄，提出模型based算法RHC-UCRL。该算法对智能体和对抗者策略均保持乐观性，并显式区分了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性。

Result: RHC-UCRL算法在存在显式对抗动态的环境下，成功实现了次线性的遗憾界限与约束违反保证。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究显式对抗动态下安全约束强化学习的工作，为安全关键场景中处理策略性外部干扰提供了理论基础与有效算法。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied.
  Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model.
  We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.

</details>


### [17] [Awakening Dormant Experts:Counterfactual Routing to Mitigate MoE Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14246)
*Wentao Hu,Yanbo Zhai,Xiaohui Hu,Mingkuan Zhao,Shanhong yu,Xue Liu,Kaidong Yu,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种免训练的推理框架CoR，通过激活稀疏MoE模型中“休眠”的长尾知识专家，在保持推理成本不变的情况下将事实准确性平均提升3.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏混合专家模型在处理长尾知识时易产生幻觉。研究发现其根源在于静态Top-k路由机制倾向于优先选择高频模式，导致掌握关键长尾知识的“专家”因门控分数低而处于休眠状态，无法在特定token上发挥其因果重要性。

Method: 提出反事实路由框架。该方法无需训练，结合层级扰动分析与反事实专家影响（CEI）指标，在保持总激活数量恒定的前提下，通过虚拟消融动态地将计算资源从语法主导层转移到知识密集型层，从而唤醒并检索具有因果决定性的休眠专家。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FACTOR和TriviaQA数据集上的广泛实验表明，CoR在不增加推理预算的情况下，将事实准确性平均提高了3.1%，建立了优于静态扩展策略的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: CoR证明了通过动态资源分配激活休眠专家的有效性，为提升稀疏MoE模型的事实可靠性提供了一种高效的、无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable scalability, yet they remain vulnerable to hallucinations, particularly when processing long-tail knowledge. We identify that this fragility stems from static Top-$k$ routing: routers tend to favor high-frequency patterns over rare factual associations. Consequently, ``specialist experts'' possessing critical long-tail knowledge are often assigned low gating scores and remain ``dormant'' -- under-prioritized for specific tokens despite their proven causal importance on other inputs. To address this, we propose Counterfactual Routing (CoR), a training-free inference framework designed to awaken these dormant experts. CoR integrates layer-wise perturbation analysis with the Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI) metric to dynamically shift computational resources from syntax-dominant to knowledge-intensive layers while maintaining a constant total activation count, effectively retrieving causally decisive experts via virtual ablation. Extensive experiments on TruthfulQA, FACTOR, and TriviaQA demonstrate that CoR improves factual accuracy by 3.1\% on average without increasing the inference budget, establishing a superior Pareto frontier compared to static scaling strategies.

</details>


### [18] [Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA):A Unified Framework for Scale-Invariant Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14249)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了度量感知主成分分析（MAPCA）框架，通过广义特征问题统一了尺度不变表示学习，揭示了不同自监督学习方法的几何本质差异，特别是发现了W-MSE与Barlow Twins在光谱方向上的对立关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法（如PCA、白化、Barlow Twins、VICReg、W-MSE等）缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们的内在联系和本质差异，特别是尺度不变性条件和输入/输出白化的区别在损失函数层面难以辨识，需要一个几何语言来统一理解这些方法。

Method: 提出MAPCA框架，核心是广义特征问题max Tr(W^T ΣW) s.t. W^T MW = I，其中M是对称正定度量矩阵。通过选择不同的M来控制表示几何：(1)β-族M(β)=Σ^β提供PCA(β=0)到输出白化(β=1)的连续谱偏置控制；(2)对角度量M=D恢复不变PCA(IPCA)；(3)证明了尺度不变性当且仅当度量在重缩放下满足M̃=CMC变换。

Result: 理论分析表明：(1)条件数κ(β)=(λ₁/λₚ)^(1-β)单调递减至各向同性；(2)IPCA是β-族框架中唯一满足精确尺度不变性的方法；(3)统一了多个自监督目标：Barlow Twins和ZCA白化对应β=1，VICReg方差项对应对角度量，W-MSE对应β=-1（输入白化），与Barlow Twins在光谱方向上完全相反。

Conclusion: MAPCA提供了一个统一的几何框架，不仅连接了经典PCA与现代自监督学习方法，更重要的是揭示了输入白化与输出白化的本质区别——这一区别在损失函数层面不可见，只有在MAPCA框架下才能精确刻画，为理解和设计新的表示学习方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We introduce Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA), a unified framework for scale-invariant representation learning based on the generalised eigenproblem max Tr(W^T Sigma W) subject to W^T M W = I, where M is a symmetric positive definite metric matrix. The choice of M determines the representation geometry. The canonical beta-family M(beta) = Sigma^beta, beta in [0,1], provides continuous spectral bias control between standard PCA (beta=0) and output whitening (beta=1), with condition number kappa(beta) = (lambda_1/lambda_p)^(1-beta) decreasing monotonically to isotropy. The diagonal metric M = D = diag(Sigma) recovers Invariant PCA (IPCA), a method rooted in Frisch (1928) diagonal regression, as a distinct member of the broader framework. We prove that scale invariance holds if and only if the metric transforms as M_tilde = CMC under rescaling C, a condition satisfied exactly by IPCA but not by the general beta-family at intermediate values.
  Beyond its classical interpretation, MAPCA provides a geometric language that unifies several self-supervised learning objectives. Barlow Twins and ZCA whitening correspond to beta=1 (output whitening); VICReg's variance term corresponds to the diagonal metric. A key finding is that W-MSE, despite being described as a whitening-based method, corresponds to M = Sigma^{-1} (beta = -1), outside the spectral compression range entirely and in the opposite spectral direction to Barlow Twins. This distinction between input and output whitening is invisible at the level of loss functions and becomes precise only within the MAPCA framework.

</details>


### [19] [Calibrate-Then-Delegate: Safety Monitoring with Risk and Budget Guarantees via Model Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14251)
*Edoardo Pona,Milad Kazemi,Mehran Hosseini,Yali Du,David Watson,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了校准-委托(CTD)框架，通过轻量级委托价值(DV)探针预测升级收益，替代基于不确定性的委托方法，在有限样本委托率保证下实现了更优的成本-准确率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全监控级联方法基于探针不确定性进行委托决策，但不确定性是委托收益的差劲代理，因为它忽略了专家是否真能纠正错误。大规模监控需要在成本和准确性之间取得平衡：廉价的潜在空间探针可以筛选每个输入，但困难案例应升级给更昂贵的专家。

Method: 提出了校准-委托(CTD)模型级联方法。核心是设计了一种新型委托价值(DV)探针——一个在与安全探针相同的内部表示上操作的轻量级模型，能够直接预测升级收益。通过多重假设检验在校准数据上校准DV信号阈值，以强制执行预算约束，从而获得委托率的有限样本保证。

Result: 在四个安全数据集上的评估表明，CTD在每个预算水平上都持续优于基于不确定性的委托方法。CTD避免了有害的过度委托，并且能够在不需要群体标签的情况下根据输入难度自适应地分配预算。该方法提供了实例级(流式)决策能力，并在计算成本上提供了概率保证。

Conclusion: CTD成功解决了大规模LLM安全监控中的成本-准确性权衡问题，通过直接预测委托收益而非依赖不确定性代理，实现了更智能的委托决策。该方法提供了理论保证和实际性能提升，为构建高效、可靠的LLM安全监控系统开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Monitoring LLM safety at scale requires balancing cost and accuracy: a cheap latent-space probe can screen every input, but hard cases should be escalated to a more expensive expert. Existing cascades delegate based on probe uncertainty, but uncertainty is a poor proxy for delegation benefit, as it ignores whether the expert would actually correct the error. To address this problem, we introduce Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD), a model-cascade approach that provides probabilistic guarantees on the computation cost while enabling instance-level (streaming) decisions. CTD builds on a novel delegation value (DV) probe, a lightweight model operating on the same internal representations as the safety probe that directly predicts the benefit of escalation. To enforce budget constraints, CTD calibrates a threshold on the DV signal using held-out data via multiple hypothesis testing, yielding finite-sample guarantees on the delegation rate. Evaluated on four safety datasets, CTD consistently outperforms uncertainty-based delegation at every budget level, avoids harmful over-delegation, and adapts budget allocation to input difficulty without requiring group labels.

</details>


### [20] [GUI-Perturbed: Domain Randomization Reveals Systematic Brittleness in GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14262)
*Yangyue Wang,Harshvardhan Sikka,Yash Mathur,Tony Zhou,Jinu Nyachhyon,Pranav Guruprasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有GUI定位模型在标准基准上表现优异，但在需要空间推理的任务中准确率大幅下降。本文提出GUI-Perturbed框架，通过独立扰动视觉场景和指令来诊断模型能力缺陷，发现关系型指令会导致模型准确率崩溃，且特定微调方法反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI定位基准测试存在缺陷，仅用单一固定指令评估每个截图，未能暴露模型在空间推理等方面的不足，导致模型在复杂指令场景下性能显著下降。

Method: 提出GUI-Perturbed控制扰动框架，独立地变化视觉场景（如浏览器缩放）和指令（如关系型指令），以细粒度地评估GUI定位模型的鲁棒性。对三种同架构的7B模型进行了系统评估，并测试了rank-8 LoRA微调及数据增强的效果。

Result: 关系型指令导致所有模型准确率系统性崩溃；70%的浏览器缩放产生统计显著的性能下降；使用增强数据的rank-8 LoRA微调反而降低了模型性能，而非提升。该框架能有效隔离并诊断空间推理、视觉鲁棒性、推理校准等具体能力轴上的问题。

Conclusion: GUI-Perturbed框架能够提供聚合基准无法提供的细粒度诊断信号，揭示了当前模型在空间推理等方面的关键缺陷。研究发布了相关数据集、增强流程和微调模型，以推动GUI定位模型鲁棒性的进一步研究。

Abstract: GUI grounding models report over 85% accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet drop 27-56 percentage points when instructions require spatial reasoning rather than direct element naming. Current benchmarks miss this because they evaluate each screenshot once with a single fixed instruction. We introduce GUI-Perturbed, a controlled perturbation framework that independently varies visual scenes and instructions to measure grounding robustness. Evaluating three 7B models from the same architecture lineage, we find that relational instructions cause systematic accuracy collapse across all models, a 70% browser zoom produces statistically significant degradation, and rank-8 LoRA fine-tuning with augmented data degrades performance rather than improving it. By perturbing along independent axes, GUI-Perturbed isolates which specific capability axes are affected-spatial reasoning, visual robustness, reasoning calibration-providing diagnostic signal that aggregate benchmarks cannot. We release the dataset, augmentation pipeline, and a fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [21] [Reinforcement Learning via Value Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14265)
*Haoran Xu,Kaiwen Hu,Somayeh Sojoudi,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了价值梯度流（VGF），一种用于行为正则化强化学习的可扩展新范式。通过将问题转化为最优传输问题，VGF避免了显式策略参数化，在离线RL和LLM微调任务上取得了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在行为正则化强化学习（如离线RL和LLM微调）中，需要通过向参考分布施加正则化来防止由错误的分布外推断引起的价值过优化问题。现有方法要么依赖重参数化策略梯度（难以扩展到大模型），要么依赖拒绝采样（过于保守），存在可扩展性或性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出价值梯度流（VGF）：1）将行为正则化RL建模为最优传输问题，目标是将参考分布映射到价值诱导的最优策略分布；2）通过离散梯度流求解该传输问题，利用价值梯度引导从参考分布初始化的粒子进行演化；3）通过控制传输预算隐式施加正则化。该方法消除了显式策略参数化，同时保持表达能力和灵活性，支持测试时自适应缩放。

Result: VGF在多个基准测试上显著优于先前方法：在离线RL基准（D4RL、OGBench）和LLM强化学习任务上均达到最先进（SOTA）结果。实验证明该方法具有良好的可扩展性和性能优势。

Conclusion: VGF为行为正则化强化学习提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，通过最优传输视角和离散梯度流，成功解决了显式策略参数化的可扩展性问题，同时避免了拒绝采样的保守性。该方法为离线RL和大模型微调提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We study behavior-regularized reinforcement learning (RL), where regularization toward a reference distribution (the dataset in offline RL or the base model in LLM RL finetuning) is essential to prevent value over-optimization caused by erroneous out-of-distribution extrapolation. Existing methods either rely on reparameterized policy gradient, which are difficult to scale to large generative models, or on reject sampling, which can be overly conservative when attempting to move beyond the behavior support. In this paper, we propose Value Gradient Flow (VGF), a scalable new paradigm for behavior-regularized RL. VGF casts behavior-regularized RL as an optimal transport problem that maps the reference distribution to the value-induced optimal policy distribution. We solve this transport problem via discrete gradient flow, where value gradients guide particles initialized from the reference distribution. Our analysis shows that VGF imposes regularization implicitly by controlling the transport budget. VGF eliminates explicit policy parameterization while remaining expressive and flexible, this enables adaptive test-time scaling by adjusting the transport budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VGF significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving state-of-the-art results on offline RL benchmarks (D4RL, OGBench) and LLM RL tasks. Code and runs can be found at https://ryanxhr.github.io/vgf.

</details>


### [22] [Enhancing LLM-based Search Agents via Contribution Weighted Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14267)
*Junzhe Wang,Zhiheng Xi,yajie yang,Hao Luo,Shihan Dou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CW-GRPO框架，通过LLM评估者生成每轮贡献分数来重新缩放基于结果的advantage，将过程监督融入GRPO，解决了过程监督价值估计不稳定和结果监督信用分配困难的问题，在Qwen3模型上性能提升5.0%-6.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体的强化训练方法存在关键局限：过程监督常受价值估计不稳定困扰，而结果监督因稀疏的轨迹级奖励导致信用分配困难，需要一种方法弥合这两种方法之间的差距。

Method: 提出贡献加权GRPO（CW-GRPO）框架，利用LLM评估者评估每个搜索轮次的检索效用和推理正确性，生成每轮贡献分数，使用这些分数沿轨迹重新缩放基于结果的advantage，在不牺牲优化稳定性的前提下实现细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在多个知识密集型基准测试上，CW-GRPO在Qwen3-8B上超越标准GRPO 5.0%，在Qwen3-1.7B上超越6.3%，带来更有效的搜索行为；分析显示成功轨迹在各轮次中呈现集中贡献特征。

Conclusion: CW-GRPO成功地将过程监督融入组相对策略优化，实现了过程监督和结果监督的优势互补，为搜索智能体任务提供了稳定的训练框架和有效的信用分配机制。

Abstract: Search agents extend Large Language Models (LLMs) beyond static parametric knowledge by enabling access to up-to-date and long-tail information unavailable during pretraining. While reinforcement learning has been widely adopted for training such agents, existing approaches face key limitations: process supervision often suffers from unstable value estimation, whereas outcome supervision struggles with credit assignment due to sparse, trajectory-level rewards. To bridge this gap, we propose Contribution-Weighted GRPO (CW-GRPO), a framework that integrates process supervision into group relative policy optimization. Instead of directly optimizing process rewards, CW-GRPO employs an LLM judge to assess the retrieval utility and reasoning correctness at each search round, producing per-round contribution scores. These scores are used to rescale outcome-based advantages along the trajectory, enabling fine-grained credit assignment without sacrificing optimization stability. Experiments on multiple knowledge-intensive benchmarks show that CW-GRPO outperforms standard GRPO by 5.0\% on Qwen3-8B and 6.3\% on Qwen3-1.7B, leading to more effective search behaviors. Additional analysis reveals that successful trajectories exhibit concentrated contributions across rounds, providing empirical insight into search agent tasks.

</details>


### [23] [Quantum-inspired tensor networks in machine learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14287)
*Guillermo Valverde,Igor García-Olaizola,Giannicola Scarpa,Alejandro Pozas-Kerstjens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统回顾了张量网络在机器学习中的应用，评估了其在计算效率、可解释性和隐私性方面的潜力与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络最初在多体物理中用于压缩表示多粒子量子态，能够通过仅捕获最相关的依赖关系来缓解多体系统的指数复杂性。由于量子纠缠与统计相关性在形式上的相似性，研究者希望将多体物理中发展出的张量网络理论理解引入机器学习，以期获得在计算效率、可解释性或隐私性方面具有优势的新方法。

Method: 本文采用综述的形式，系统回顾了张量网络在机器学习领域的应用现状，分析了张量网络作为替代学习架构以及作为神经网络组件分解的两种主要运作模式。

Result: 文章提供了对张量网络在机器学习中应用现状的批判性评估，总结了该领域目前的发展水平，并指出了其在计算效率、可解释性和隐私保护等方面的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 文章指出，尽管张量网络在机器学习中具有显著潜力，但仍需克服一系列挑战才能充分发挥其优势，未来的研究需要致力于解决这些挑战以推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Tensor networks were developed in the context of many-body physics as compressed representations of multiparticle quantum states. These representations mitigate the exponential complexity of many-body systems by capturing only the most relevant dependencies. Due to the formal similarity between quantum entanglement and statistical correlations, tensor networks have recently been integrated in machine learning, operating both as alternative learning architectures and as decompositions of components of neural networks. The expectation is that the theoretical understanding of tensor networks developed within quantum many-body physics leads to novel methods that offer advantages in terms of computational efficiency, explainability, or privacy. Here we review the use of tensor networks in the context of machine learning, providing a critical assessment of the state of the art, the potential advantages, and the challenges that must be overcome.

</details>


### [24] [Heat and Matérn Kernels on Matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14331)
*Dmitry Eremeev,Salem Said,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为匹配问题构建了几何核函数框架，提出了基于带状多项式的次指数算法解决计算难题，并发现该方法无法直接迁移至系统发育树空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于匹配具有离散、非欧几里得的特性，将核方法应用于匹配面临巨大挑战。现有方法难以自然地处理匹配空间的几何结构，因此需要开发一个能够尊重匹配空间固有几何特性的原则性框架。

Method: 首先完整刻画了匹配空间上的平稳核；在此基础上重点研究热核和Matérn核族，为平稳性引入平滑性的归纳偏置；针对朴素评估的超指数计算代价，提出利用带状多项式的新型次指数算法实现高效核评估。

Result: 成功将欧氏空间中广泛使用的热核和Matérn核扩展至匹配空间；所提算法将核评估的计算复杂度从超指数降低至次指数级别；发现该框架无法无缝迁移至系统发育树空间，建立了否定性结果。

Conclusion: 本研究为匹配问题提供了首个尊重其自然几何结构的核方法框架，在理论与计算效率上均取得突破。树空间的否定性结果揭示了该方法论的适用边界，并为未来研究指明了重要开放问题。

Abstract: Applying kernel methods to matchings is challenging due to their discrete, non-Euclidean nature. In this paper, we develop a principled framework for constructing geometric kernels that respect the natural geometry of the space of matchings. To this end, we first provide a complete characterization of stationary kernels, i.e. kernels that respect the inherent symmetries of this space. Because the class of stationary kernels is too broad, we specifically focus on the heat and Matérn kernel families, adding an appropriate inductive bias of smoothness to stationarity. While these families successfully extend widely popular Euclidean kernels to matchings, evaluating them naively incurs a prohibitive super-exponential computational cost. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce and analyze a novel, sub-exponential algorithm leveraging zonal polynomials for efficient kernel evaluation. Finally, motivated by the known bijective correspondence between matchings and phylogenetic trees-a crucial data modality in biology-we explore whether our framework can be seamlessly transferred to the space of trees, establishing novel negative results and identifying a significant open problem.

</details>

*Aditi De*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了基于热力学原理的生产级扩散模型推理，通过分层双线性耦合和极简数字接口克服了非局部跳跃连接和输入条件化两大障碍，在保持0.9906余弦相似度的同时，理论上可节省约$10^7$倍的能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理与过阻尼Langevin动力学在形式上相同，因此可以通过物理基质编码分数函数，仅凭热力学即可达到平衡输出，无需数字算术运算，相对GPU可能实现$10,000\times$的能耗降低。然而，非局部跳跃连接和输入条件化两大基本障碍一直阻碍了该等效性在生产规模上的实现。

Method: 提出了两种创新解决方案：(1) 分层双线性耦合，将U-Net跳跃连接编码为rank-$k$模块间交互，仅需$O(Dk)$物理连接而非$O(D^2)$；(2) 极简数字接口，包含4维瓶颈编码器和16单元传输网络，总计仅2,560个参数，克服了条件化障碍。

Result: 在训练好的去噪U-Net激活值上评估，完整系统达到解码器余弦相似度0.9906（上限为1.0000），同时保持相对于GPU推理约$10^7\times$的理论净能量节省。

Conclusion: 这是首次实现训练权重的生产级热力学扩散推理的演示，证明了基于物理基质的扩散模型推理在实际应用中的可行性，为超低能耗AI推理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Diffusion-model inference and overdamped Langevin dynamics are formally identical. A physical substrate that encodes the score function therefore equilibrates to the correct output by thermodynamics alone, requiring no digital arithmetic during inference and potentially achieving a $10{,}000\times$ reduction in energy relative to a GPU. Two fundamental barriers have until now prevented this equivalence from being realized at production scale: non-local skip connections, which locally coupled analog substrates cannot represent, and input conditioning, in which the coupling constants carry roughly $2{,}600\times$ too little signal to anchor the system to a specific input.
  We resolve both obstacles. \emph{Hierarchical bilinear coupling} encodes U-Net skip connections as rank-$k$ inter-module interactions derived directly from the singular structure of the encoder and decoder Gram matrices, requiring only $O(Dk)$ physical connections instead of $O(D^2)$. A \emph{minimal digital interface} -- a 4-dimensional bottleneck encoder together with a 16-unit transfer network, totalling \textbf{2,560 parameters} -- overcomes the conditioning barrier. When evaluated on activations drawn from a trained denoising U-Net, the complete system attains a decoder cosine similarity of \textbf{0.9906} against an oracle upper bound of 1.0000, while preserving theoretical net energy savings of approximately $10^7\times$ over GPU inference. These results constitute the first demonstration of trained-weight, production-scale thermodynamic diffusion inference.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的KOL投资建议被广泛用作投资指导，但这些言论通常只表达方向性意图（买什么/卖什么及原因），而系统性地缺失执行决策（何时、多少、多久），如何将这些不完整的建议转化为可执行的交易策略而不引入额外假设仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出意图保留策略补全框架，将KOL言论视为部分交易策略，利用离线强化学习补全围绕KOL表达意图的缺失执行决策，同时保持方向性意图的一致性。

Result: 在YouTube和X平台（2022-2025）的多模态KOL言论实验中，KICL在两个平台上均取得了最佳收益和夏普比率，同时保持零无支持入场和零方向反转，消融实验显示完整框架相比KOL对齐基线实现18.9%的收益提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功弥合了KOL投资建议与可执行交易策略之间的鸿沟，通过意图保留的强化学习方法，在忠实于原始投资意图的同时显著提升了策略表现。

Abstract: Key Opinion Leader (KOL) discourse on social media is widely consumed as investment guidance, yet turning it into executable trading strategies without injecting assumptions about unspecified execution decisions remains an open problem. We observe that the gaps in KOL statements are not random deficiencies but a structured separation: KOLs express directional intent (what to buy or sell and why) while leaving execution decisions (when, how much, how long) systematically unspecified. Building on this observation, we propose an intent-preserving policy completion framework that treats KOL discourse as a partial trading policy and uses offline reinforcement learning to complete the missing execution decisions around the KOL-expressed intent. Experiments on multimodal KOL discourse from YouTube and X (2022-2025) show that KICL achieves the best return and Sharpe ratio on both platforms while maintaining zero unsupported entries and zero directional reversals, and ablations confirm that the full framework yields an 18.9% return improvement over the KOL-aligned baseline.

</details>


### [27] [Path-Sampled Integrated Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14338)
*Firuz Kamalov,Fadi Thabtah,R. Sivaraj,Neda Abdelhamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了路径采样积分梯度(PS-IG)框架，将特征归因计算推广为沿线性插值路径采样的基线期望值。该方法在数学上等价于路径加权积分梯度，将平滑模型的误差收敛率从O(m^{-1/2})提升至O(m^{-1})，并作为梯度噪声的方差缩减滤波器，在均匀采样下严格将归因方差降低1/3倍，同时保持线性和实现不变性等公理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征归因方法在处理基线选择和梯度噪声时存在局限性，需要提高计算的准确性和效率。现有方法在误差收敛率和方差控制方面存在不足，迫切需要一种既能保持公理性质又能提升计算性能的通用框架。

Method: 提出路径采样积分梯度(PS-IG)框架，通过沿线性插值路径采样基线并计算期望值来泛化特征归因。证明了PS-IG与路径加权积分梯度的数学等价性，建立了加权函数与采样密度累积分布函数的对应关系，从而将随机期望转化为确定性黎曼和进行计算。

Result: 理论证明PS-IG将平滑模型的误差收敛率从O(m^{-1/2})提升至O(m^{-1})；解析推导表明PS-IG作为方差缩减滤波器，在均匀采样下严格将归因方差降低1/3倍，有效抑制梯度噪声对归因结果的影响。

Conclusion: PS-IG框架成功地将随机采样与确定性计算相结合，在保持线性和实现不变性等关键公理性质的同时，显著提升了特征归因的计算精度和稳定性，为可解释机器学习提供了一种更高效、更可靠的归因方法。

Abstract: We introduce path-sampled integrated gradients (PS-IG), a framework that generalizes feature attribution by computing the expected value over baselines sampled along the linear interpolation path. We prove that PS-IG is mathematically equivalent to path-weighted integrated gradients, provided the weighting function matches the cumulative distribution function of the sampling density. This equivalence allows the stochastic expectation to be evaluated via a deterministic Riemann sum, improving the error convergence rate from $O(m^{-1/2})$ to $O(m^{-1})$ for smooth models. Furthermore, we demonstrate analytically that PS-IG functions as a variance-reducing filter against gradient noise - strictly lowering attribution variance by a factor of 1/3 under uniform sampling - while preserving key axiomatic properties such as linearity and implementation invariance.

</details>

*Tianhao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自主推理中的节点扩展建模为存在系统偏差的局部最佳臂识别问题，推导出了安全剪枝的可行性条件与样本复杂度上下界，并验证了该方法能有效保留最优路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主推理和具身规划中，随着搜索深度增加，候选动作空间呈指数级膨胀。常用的启发式剪枝方法在 surrogate 模型（如 LLM）存在系统性评估偏差时缺乏形式化的安全保证，可能导致最优动作被错误剔除。

Method: 将节点扩展过程形式化为动态前沿上的局部最佳臂识别（BAI）问题，设定系统偏差上界为 $L$。通过反演 Lambert W 函数推导出样本复杂度上界，并给出信息论下界，确定了当经验奖励间隙超过 $4L$ 时安全节点消除才可行。

Result: 建立了 $\mathcal{O}((Δ-4L)^{-2})$ 的加性样本复杂度上界和 $Ω((Δ-2L)^{-2})$ 的下界，证明了安全搜索的结构性限制。在合成树和复杂推理任务上的实验表明，遵守该局部安全边界能有效保留最优轨迹，同时最大化样本分配效率。

Conclusion: 在存在系统性偏差的搜索场景中，必须在经验奖励间隙足够大时才可安全剪枝；本文提出的理论边界为启发式搜索提供了形式化的安全保障，实现了安全性与效率的平衡。

Abstract: As search depth increases in autonomous reasoning and embodied planning, the candidate action space expands exponentially, heavily taxing computational budgets. While heuristic pruning is a common countermeasure, it operates without formal safety guarantees when surrogate models (like LLMs) exhibit systematic evaluation biases. This paper frames the node expansion process as a localized Best-Arm Identification (BAI) problem over dynamic frontiers, subject to a bounded systematic bias $L$. By inverting the Lambert W function, we establish an additive sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}((Δ-4L)^{-2})$, which indicates that safe node elimination is only feasible when the empirical reward gap exceeds $4L$. We complement this with an information-theoretic lower bound of $Ω((Δ-2L)^{-2})$ to confirm the structural limits of biased search. Subsequent evaluations on both synthetic trees and complex reasoning tasks demonstrate that adhering to this local safety boundary successfully preserves optimal trajectories while maximizing sample allocation efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [Modular Continual Learning via Zero-Leakage Reconstruction Routing and Autonomous Task Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14375)
*Noureddine Kermiche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种硅原生模块化架构，通过任务特定专家和门控机制实现参数隔离，采用并行流水线解决灾难性遗忘问题，同时满足GDPR隐私合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难性遗忘是神经网络序列任务学习的主要障碍，现有方法无法有效平衡知识保留与隐私合规（如GDPR）的需求。

Method: 设计Simultaneous Pipeline并行执行Teacher学习、Student蒸馏和Router流形获取；提出Tight-Bottleneck Autoencoder (TB-AE)解决高维潜在空间拥挤问题；建立Autonomous Retrieval机制识别返回流形。

Result: TB-AE有效区分4096维LLM嵌入中的语义拥挤流形；Live Distillation作为自然正则化器，在CV和NLP领域实现强知识保留，无学生模型保真度差距。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结构化参数隔离和并行训练范式，成功实现了计算高效、隐私友好的终身学习，为解决灾难性遗忘提供了新途径。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a primary hurdle in sequential task learning for artificial neural networks. We propose a silicon-native modular architecture that achieves structural parameter isolation using Task-Specific Experts and a distributed, outlier-based Gatekeeper. Moving beyond traditional sequential consolidation, our framework utilizes a Simultaneous Pipeline where Teacher learning, Student distillation, and Router manifold acquisition occur in parallel while raw data is present in a localized training session. This approach ensures computational efficiency and complies with privacy mandates like GDPR by deleting raw data as soon as a task is learned. We demonstrate that a Tight-Bottleneck Autoencoder (TB-AE) can effectively distinguish semantically crowded manifolds in high-dimensional latent spaces, overcoming the posterior collapse inherent to standard variational methods. By establishing strict topological boundaries, our TB-AE resolves latent space crowding in 4096-D LLM embeddings to provide a robust, unsupervised novelty signal. Furthermore, we validate an Autonomous Retrieval mechanism that confidently identifies returning manifolds, enabling stable lifelong learning without redundant module instantiation. Empirical results demonstrate that our ``Live Distillation'' approach acts as a natural regularizer, achieving strong retention across computer vision and natural language processing domains without suffering a student fidelity gap.

</details>


### [30] [Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment with Multiple Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14379)
*Qi Zhang,Dawei Wang,Shaofeng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MSDDA框架，无需重新训练即可实现扩散模型的多目标对齐，且无近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理扩散模型的多目标对齐时存在成本高、需要奖励值或梯度信息、引入近似误差等问题。人类偏好本质上是多元的，对齐模型需要平衡多个下游目标（如美学质量和文本-图像一致性），因此需要一种高效且精确的多目标对齐方法。

Method: 提出步骤级强化学习公式，将多目标对齐问题转化为去噪时间优化问题。构建MSDDA框架，以闭式形式获得最优反向去噪分布，其均值和方差直接由单目标基础模型表示，无需重新训练。理论上证明了该去噪时间目标与步骤级RL微调完全等价。

Result: 理论证明MSDDA框架引入零近似误差。数值实验表明该方法优于现有的去噪时间方法。

Conclusion: MSDDA为扩散模型多目标对齐提供了一种无需重新训练的理论完备框架，兼具效率与精确性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful tool for aligning diffusion models with human preferences, typically by optimizing a single reward function under a KL regularization constraint. In practice, however, human preferences are inherently pluralistic, and aligned models must balance multiple downstream objectives, such as aesthetic quality and text-image consistency. Existing multi-objective approaches either rely on costly multi-objective RL fine-tuning or on fusing separately aligned models at denoising time, but they generally require access to reward values (or their gradients) and/or introduce approximation error in the resulting denoising objectives. In this paper, we revisit the problem of RL fine-tuning for diffusion models and address the intractability of identifying the optimal policy by introducing a step-level RL formulation. Building on this, we further propose Multi-objective Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment (MSDDA), a retraining-free framework for aligning diffusion models with multiple objectives, obtaining the optimal reverse denoising distribution in closed form, with mean and variance expressed directly in terms of single-objective base models. We prove that this denoising-time objective is exactly equivalent to the step-level RL fine-tuning, introducing no approximation error. Moreover, we provide numerical results, which indicate our method outperforms existing denoising-time approaches.

</details>

*Sudeepta Mondal,Soumalya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Koopman自编码器的物理信息时空代理建模框架（PISTM），用于高效求解非线性时空动力学系统，在圆柱绕流问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计中的多物理场仿真虽然能捕捉精细时空尺度，但计算成本极高，成为端到端设计流程的瓶颈。现有的纯数据驱动代理模型虽能加速仿真，但在训练分布外的输入上泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出物理信息时空代理建模（PISTM）框架，结合Koopman自编码器以非侵入方式学习底层时空动力学，并耦合时空代理模型，用于预测指定时间窗口内未知工况下Koopman算子的行为。

Result: 在二维不可压缩圆柱绕流这一典型流体力学问题上对所提框架进行了评估与验证。

Conclusion: 通过将物理约束融入代理模型，PISTM框架有望提升模型在训练分布外的泛化能力，为解决高保真仿真计算成本高昂的问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Most practical engineering design problems involve nonlinear spatio-temporal dynamical systems. Multi-physics simulations are often performed to capture the fine spatio-temporal scales which govern the evolution of these systems. However, these simulations are often high-fidelity in nature, and can be computationally very expensive. Hence, generating data from these expensive simulations becomes a bottleneck in an end-to-end engineering design process. Spatio-temporal surrogate modeling of these dynamical systems has been a popular data-driven solution to tackle this computational bottleneck. This is because accurate machine learning models emulating the dynamical systems can be orders of magnitude faster than the actual simulations. However, one key limitation of purely data-driven approaches is their lack of generalizability to inputs outside the training distribution. In this paper, we propose a physics-informed spatio-temporal surrogate modeling (PISTM) framework constrained by the physics of the underlying dynamical system. The framework leverages state-of-the-art advancements in the field of Koopman autoencoders to learn the underlying spatio-temporal dynamics in a non-intrusive manner, coupled with a spatio-temporal surrogate model which predicts the behavior of the Koopman operator in a specified time window for unknown operating conditions. We evaluate our framework on a prototypical fluid flow problem of interest: two-dimensional incompressible flow around a cylinder.

</details>


### [32] [Asynchronous Probability Ensembling for Federated Disaster Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14450)
*Emanuel Teixeira Martins,Rodrigo Moreira,Larissa Ferreira Rodrigues Moreira,Rodolfo S. Villaça,Augusto Neto,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于异步概率聚合和反馈蒸馏的去中心化集成框架，通过交换类别概率向量而非模型权重，在保护数据隐私的同时大幅降低通信开销并提升灾难图像识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难决策支持系统（DDSS）中的应急处理受限于网络延迟和应用准确率欠佳。现有联邦学习（FL）方法虽有所助益，但在异构卷积神经网络（CNN）架构间仍面临高昂通信成本和严格同步要求的约束，难以满足快速、准确的实时灾难响应需求。

Method: 提出一种去中心化集成框架，将交互单元从模型权重转变为类别概率向量；采用异步概率聚合机制，使异构CNN架构能够异步协作；引入反馈蒸馏技术以进一步提升模型性能。该方法在保持数据隐私的同时，显著降低了通信需求。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在灾难图像识别任务上优于传统独立骨干网络和标准联邦学习方法，能够在资源受限环境下有效提升识别性能，并将通信需求降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 本研究为实时灾难响应提供了一种可扩展且具备资源感知能力的解决方案，有效解决了异构CNN协作中的通信与同步瓶颈，在保持隐私的同时实现了更优的准确性与效率。

Abstract: Quick and accurate emergency handling in Disaster Decision Support Systems (DDSS) is often hampered by network latency and suboptimal application accuracy. While Federated Learning (FL) addresses some of these issues, it is constrained by high communication costs and rigid synchronization requirements across heterogeneous convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes a decentralized ensembling framework based on asynchronous probability aggregation and feedback distillation. By shifting the exchange unit from model weights to class-probability vectors, our method maintains data privacy, reduces communication requirements by orders of magnitude, and improves overall accuracy. This approach enables diverse CNN designs to collaborate asynchronously, enhancing disaster image identification performance even in resource-constrained settings. Experimental tests demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms traditional individual backbones and standard federated approaches, establishing a scalable and resource-aware solution for real-time disaster response.

</details>


### [33] [Auxiliary Finite-Difference Residual-Gradient Regularization for PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14472)
*Stavros Kassinos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合PINN架构，在保持AD计算PDE残差的基础上，引入仅作辅助正则化的有限差分（FD）项来惩罚残差场的梯度，从而在特定物理量（如壁面热通量）上显著提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN通常仅使用单一标量损失进行优化，难以针对特定物理量进行精细化控制，导致在复杂几何边界附近的预测精度不足。作者希望设计一种混合方法，在不牺牲AD优势的前提下，通过辅助的正则化手段定向提升物理量的预测准确性。

Method: 提出一种混合PINN框架：PDE残差仍基于自动微分（AD）计算，同时引入基于有限差分（FD）的弱辅助项，用于惩罚采样残差场的梯度。该方法在两阶段进行验证：阶段一采用受控Poisson基准问题，对比基线PINN、FD残差梯度正则化器及匹配的AD残差梯度基线；阶段二将逻辑迁移至三维环形热传导问题（PINN3D），在波纹外壁附近采用体拟合壳层网格实现辅助正则化。

Result: 在阶段一，FD正则化器重现了残差梯度控制的主要效果，并揭示了场精度与残差清洁度之间的权衡关系。在阶段二，壳层正则化显著改善了面向应用的物理量：在Kourkoutas-beta优化器下，固定壳权重为5e-4时，外壁边界条件RMSE从1.22e-2降至9.29e-4，壁面热通量RMSE从9.21e-3降至9.63e-4。Adam优化器在初始学习率降至1e-3时可用，但其壳层正则化效果不如Kourkoutas-beta稳健。

Conclusion: 研究支持了一种针对性的混合PINN观点：当辅助FD正则化项与所关注的物理量（如外壁热通量）相一致时，其价值最大。该方法为工程应用中特定物理量的精确预测提供了一种有效的混合策略。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are often selected by a single scalar loss even when the quantity of interest is more specific. We study a hybrid design in which the governing PDE residual remains automatic-differentiation (AD) based, while finite differences (FD) appear only in a weak auxiliary term that penalizes gradients of the sampled residual field. The FD term regularizes the residual field without replacing the PDE residual itself.
  We examine this idea in two stages. Stage 1 is a controlled Poisson benchmark comparing a baseline PINN, the FD residual-gradient regularizer, and a matched AD residual-gradient baseline. Stage 2 transfers the same logic to a three-dimensional annular heat-conduction benchmark (PINN3D), where baseline errors concentrate near a wavy outer wall and the auxiliary grid is implemented as a body-fitted shell adjacent to the wall.
  In Stage 1, the FD regularizer reproduces the main effect of residual-gradient control while exposing a trade-off between field accuracy and residual cleanliness. In Stage 2, the shell regularizer improves the application-facing quantities, namely outer-wall flux and boundary-condition behavior. Across seeds 0-5 and 100k epochs, the most reliable tested configuration is a fixed shell weight of 5e-4 under the Kourkoutas-beta optimizer regime: relative to a matched run without the shell term, it reduces the mean outer-wall BC RMSE from 1.22e-2 to 9.29e-4 and the mean wall-flux RMSE from 9.21e-3 to 9.63e-4. Adam with beta2=0.999 becomes usable when the initial learning rate is reduced to 1e-3, although its shell benefit is less robust than under Kourkoutas-beta. Overall, the results support a targeted view of hybrid PINNs: an auxiliary-only FD regularizer is most valuable when it is aligned with the physical quantity of interest, here the outer-wall flux.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逆向强化学习（IRL）的电竞选手选拔框架，通过学习职业选手的奖励函数，根据候选人与目标明星选手的战术风格匹配度进行排名，实现数据驱动的精准球探工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电竞球探工作流程严重依赖人工视频审查和聚合性能指标，往往无法捕捉细粒度的决策模式，难以判断候选人是否符合特定战术原型，因此需要一种能够量化战术风格并对齐目标选手的新方法。

Method: 将基于风格的选手评估建模为逆向强化学习问题。框架采用双分支多模态输入：一支路编码源自高分辨率游戏遥测数据的结构化状态-动作轨迹，另一支路编码由视觉-语言模型（VLM）从比赛直播画面生成的时序对齐战术伪解说。通过生成对抗模仿学习（GAIL）目标融合并评估这些表征，判别器学习捕捉精英职业选手独特的操作与战术特征。

Result: 所提框架能够学习职业特定的奖励函数，并依据候选人与目标明星选手的风格一致性进行排序，实现了从通用技能评估向"基于奖励"的球探模式转变。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展、融合工作流的数字孪生系统，支持跨大规模候选人池的数据驱动阵容构建和针对性人才发现，为电竞选手选拔提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Traditional esports scouting workflows rely heavily on manual video review and aggregate performance metrics, which often fail to capture the nuanced decision-making patterns necessary to determine if a prospect fits a specific tactical archetype. To address this, we reframe style-based player evaluation in esports as an Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) problem. In this paper, we introduce a novel player selection framework that learns professional-specific reward functions from logged gameplay demonstrations, allowing organizations to rank candidates by their stylistic alignment with a target star player. Our proposed architecture utilizes a multimodal, two-branch intake: one branch encodes structured state-action trajectories derived from high-resolution in-game telemetry, while the second encodes temporally aligned tactical pseudo-commentary generated by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) from broadcast footage. These representations are fused and evaluated via a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) objective, where a discriminator learns to capture the unique mechanical and tactical signatures of elite professionals. By transitioning from generic skill estimation to scouting "by reward," this framework provides a scalable, workflow-aware digital twin system that enables data-driven roster construction and targeted talent discovery across massive candidate pools.

</details>


### [35] [Quantization of Spiking Neural Networks Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14487)
*Evan Gibson Smith,Jacob Whitehill,Fatemeh Ganji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出SNN量化评估不应仅关注精度，还需关注发放行为的保持；提出使用EMD作为诊断指标来度量量化前后发放分布的差异，发现均匀量化会导致分布漂移，而LQ-Net式学习量化能更好地保持原始发放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脉冲神经网络（SNN）量化评估几乎只关注准确率，忽略了量化后的网络是否保持了全精度网络的发放行为。然而，发放活动决定了部署时的有效稀疏性、状态存储和事件处理负载，因此准确率相同但发放分布不同的模型在实际部署中表现会有差异。

Method: 提出使用推土机距离（Earth Mover's Distance, EMD）作为诊断指标来度量量化网络与全精度网络之间发放分布的 divergence。在SEW-ResNet架构上，针对CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集，系统性地评估了不同量化方法（均匀量化和LQ-Net式学习量化）、裁剪范围和位宽对权重和膜电位量化效果的影响。

Result: 实验发现，即使准确率得以保持，均匀量化仍会引起发放分布的漂移；相比之下，LQ-Net风格的学习量化方法能够使发放行为保持在与全精度基线接近的状态。这表明在同等准确率下，不同量化配置会产生显著不同的发放分布。

Conclusion: 发放行为的保持应被视为与准确率同等重要的评估标准。EMD提供了一种有原则的工具来评估这一特性，有助于指导SNN量化方法的选择和优化，确保量化模型在资源受限硬件上的实际部署效果。

Abstract: Quantization is a natural complement to the sparse, event-driven computation of Spiking Neural Networks, reducing memory bandwidth and arithmetic cost for deployment on resource-constrained hardware. However, existing SNN quantization evaluation focuses almost exclusively on accuracy, overlooking whether a quantized network preserves the firing behavior of its full-precision counterpart. We demonstrate that quantization method, clipping range, and bit-width can produce substantially different firing distributions at equivalent accuracy, differences invisible to standard metrics but relevant to deployment, where firing activity governs effective sparsity, state storage, and event-processing load. To capture this gap, we propose Earth Mover's Distance as a diagnostic metric for firing distribution divergence, and apply it systematically across weight and membrane quantization on SEW-ResNet architectures trained on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100. We find that uniform quantization induces distributional drift even when accuracy is preserved, while LQ-Net style learned quantization maintains firing behavior close to the full-precision baseline. Our results suggest that behavior preservation should be treated as an evaluation criterion alongside accuracy, and that EMD provides a principled tool for assessing it.

</details>
